total_start, 1)\\n        output = {\\\"summary\\\": summary, \\\"results\\\": all_results}\\n        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output) or \\\".\\\", exist_ok=True)\\n        with open(args.output, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, default=str)\\n\\n    # Final summary\\n    total_wall = time.time() - total_start\\n    summary = compute_summary(all_results, extractor.stats)\\n    summary[\\\"k\\\"] = args.k\\n    summary[\\\"mode\\\"] = args.mode\\n    summary[\\\"total_wall_time_s\\\"] = round(total_wall, 1)\\n\\n    output = {\\\"summary\\\": summary, \\\"results\\\": all_results}\\n    with open(args.output, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2, default=str)\\n\\n    print_summary(summary)\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nTotal wall time: {total_wall/60:.1f} min\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Results saved to {args.output}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"FULL_MVP_DONE\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.53}}"

         groups[canonical_map[key]] = []\\n        groups[canonical_map[key]].append(i)\\n    return groups\\n\\n\\nclass SICAPipeline:\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        trace_generator:  self.constraint_extractor = constraint_extractor\\n        self.k = k\\n        self.deduplicator = ConstraintDeduplicator()\\n        self.solver = MaxSATSolver()\\n        self.scorer = InvariantScorer()\\n        self.maxsat_timeout_ms = maxsat_timeout_ms\\n\\n    def run_single(self, problem: dict) -> dict:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Run full SICA pipeline on a single problem.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        t_start = time.perf_counter()\\n\\n        # Auto-detect domain from problem metadata\\n        domain = \\\"logic\\\" if problem.get(\\\"dataset\\\") == Generate K traces\\n            t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n            traces = self.trace_generator.generate(problem[\\\"problem\\\"], k=self.k)\\n            trace_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n            cached_traces = traces\\n\\n            # 2. Extract constraints from each trace (batch for concurrency)\\n            t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n            all_constraints = self.constraint_extractor.extract_batch(\\n                [t[\\\"trace\\\"] for t in traces]\\n            )\\n            extract_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n            total_constraints = sum(len(c) for c in all_constraints)\\n            non_empty = sum(1 for c in all_constraints if c)\\n\\n            # 3. Deduplicate via z3 equivalence\\n            t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n            unique_constraints = self.deduplicator.deduplicate(all_constraints)\\n            dedup_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n            # 4. MAX-SAT solve\\n            t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n            maxsat_result = self.solver.solve(unique_constraints, timeout_ms=self.maxsat_timeout_ms)\\n            maxsat_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n            # 5. Group equivalent answers, then score\\n            raw_answers = [t[\\\"answer\\\"] for t in traces if t[\\\"answer\\\"]]\\n\\n            if domain == \\\"logic\\\":\\n                equiv_groups = _group_logic_answers(raw_answers)\\n            else:\\n                equiv_groups = group_equivalent_answers(raw_answers)\\n\\n            # Remap trace answers to canonical forms for scorer\\n            canonical_map = {}\\n            for canonical, indices in equiv_groups.items():\\n                for idx in indices:\\n                    canonical_map[raw_answers[idx]] = canonical\\n\\n            for t in traces:\\n                if t[\\\"answer\\\"] in canonical_map:\\n                    t[\\\"answer\\\"] = canonical_map[t[\\\"answer\\\"]]\\n\\n            candidates = list(equiv_groups.keys())\\n            answer_counts = {k: len(v) for k, v in equiv_groups.items()}\\n            scores = self.scorer.score(maxsat_result, traces, candidates)\\n            selected = self.scorer.select_answer(scores, answer_counts)\\n\\n            total_time = time.perf_counter() - t_start\\n\\n            return {\\n                \\\"answer\\\": selected,\\n                \\\"domain\\\": domain,\\n                \\\"scores\\\": scores,\\n                \\\"answer_counts\\\": answer_counts,\\n                \\\"traces\\\": traces,\\n                \\\"constraints_stats\\\": {\\n                    \\\"total_extracted\\\": total_constraints,\\n                    \\\"traces_with_constraints\\\": non_empty,\\n                    \\\"unique_after_dedup\\\": len(unique_constraints),\\n                },\\n                \\\"maxsat_stats\\\": {\\n                    \\\"satisfied\\\": len(maxsat_result.satisfied),\\n                    \\\"excluded\\\": len(maxsat_result.excluded),\\n                    \\\"total_weight\\\": maxsat_result.total_weight,\\n                    \\\"solve_time_ms\\\": = dict(counter)\\n                    result[\\\"traces\\\"] = cached_traces\\n            except Exception:\\n                pass\\n            result[\\\"timing\\\"] = {\\\"total_s\\\": round(time.perf_counter() - t_start, 3)}\\n            return result\\n\\n    def run_batch(self, problems: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Run pipeline on a batch of problems.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        results = []\\n        for i, p in enumerate(problems):\\n            logger.info(\\\"Processing problem %d/%d\\\", i + 1, len(problems))\\n            result = Supports vLLM API, external API, and mock mode.\\nInput: math problem string, K (number of traces)\\nOutput: list of {\\\"trace\\\": str, \\\"answer\\\": str} dicts\\nDependencies: openai, re, asyncio\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport asyncio\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nimport random\\nimport logging\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\nfrom typing import Optional\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSOLVE_PROMPT = \\\"\\\"\\\"Solve the following math problem step by step. Show all your reasoning.\\nAt the end, put your final answer in \\\\\\\\boxed{{}}.\\n\\nProblem: {problem}\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_boxed_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract the last \\\\\\\\boxed{...} content from text, handling nested braces.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    idx = text.rfind('\\\\\\\\boxed{')\\n    if idx == -1:\\n        return \\\"\\\"\\n    start = idx + len('\\\\\\\\boxed{')\\n    depth = 1\\n    i = start\\n    while i < len(text) and depth > 0:\\n        if text[i] == '{':\\n            depth += 1\\n        elif text[i] == '}':\\n            depth -= 1\\n        i += 1\\n    return text[start:i-1].strip()\\n\\n\\ndef extract_answer_fallback(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fallback answer extraction when \\\\\\\\boxed{} is missing (e.g., truncated traces).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    patterns = [\\n        # \\\"the answer is X\\\" / \\\"the final answer is X\\\"\\n        r'(?:the\\\\s+)?(?:final\\\\s+)?answer\\\\s+is\\\\s*[:\\\\s]*\\\\$?\\\\\\\\?boxed\\\\{([^}]+)\\\\}',\\n        r'(?:the\\\\s+)?(?:final\\\\s+)?answer\\\\s+is\\\\s*[:\\\\s]*\\\\$?\\\\*?\\\\*?([+-]?\\\\d+(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?)\\\\*?\\\\*?\\\\$?',\\n        # \\\"therefore/thus/hence ... = X\\\"\\n        r'(?:therefore|thus|hence|so)\\\\s*,?\\\\s*[^=\\\\n]{0,80}?=\\\\s*\\\\$?\\\\*?\\\\*?([+-]?\\\\d+(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?)\\\\*?\\\\*?\\\\$?',\\n        # \\\"= X\\\" near end (last 500 chars)\\n        r'=\\\\s*\\\\$?\\\\*?\\\\*?([+-]?\\\\d+(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?)\\\\*?\\\\*?\\\\$?\\\\s*$',\\n    ]\\n\\n    # Try last 1000 chars first (final answer more likely near end), then full text\\n    for search_text in [text[-1000:], text]:\\n        for pattern in patterns:\\n            matches = re.findall(pattern, search_text, re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE)\\n            if matches:\\n                return matches[-1].strip()\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef extract_answer(text: str) -> tuple[str, str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Extract answer from trace text.
